商业与金融

BUSINESS & FINANCE · 7 stories

B1

亚马逊正在洽谈收购一家人工智能初创公司

P1 · 必读

By Kate Clark and Lauren Thomas

据知情人士透露，亚马逊正在与人工智能初创公司Decart（AI初创公司）洽谈收购事宜，交易估值可能达到约70亿美元。

总部位于旧金山的Decart的软件可以帮助开发者更轻松地在不同的AI芯片之间切换，这在各公司竞相获取更多计算能力的情况下变得越来越重要。

知情人士表示，如果谈判不破裂，或者没有出现其他竞购方，这笔交易可能会很快达成。

据PitchBook（创投数据商）的数据，Decart已从红杉资本（Sequoia Capital）、Benchmark（风投）和Radical Ventures（风投）等投资者处筹集了超过4亿美元的风险投资。该公司在5月份的估值接近40亿美元。该公司由三位以色列工程师于2023年创立，他们是兄弟迪恩·莱特斯多夫（Dean Leitersdorf）和奥里安·莱特斯多夫（Orian Leitersdorf）以及莫舍·沙列夫（Moshe Shalev）。Decart正在开发名为Lucy和Oasis的世界模型。世界模型可以生成和模拟现实世界的3D环境。

为了与英伟达竞争，亚马逊开发了自己的定制AI芯片Trainium3（亚马逊自研AI芯片），英伟达目前仍是图形处理器市场的领导者，而图形处理器是驱动人工智能发展的专用芯片。据《华尔街日报》报道，亚马逊网络服务（AWS）在与Decart会面并对其创始人印象深刻后，于2025年向该公司提供了对该芯片的早期访问权限。

近年来，亚马逊的其他重大收购包括2022年以65亿美元收购米高梅电影和电视工作室，以及今年早些时候宣布的，以约110亿美元收购星链竞争对手Globalstar（卫星通信公司，星链竞争对手）。

以色列新闻媒体Calcalist（以色列财经媒体）本月早些时候报道称，Decart正在与一家大型科技公司进行高级别的收购谈判。

Amazon.com is in talks to acquire artificial-intelligence startup Decart in a deal that could be valued at around $7 billion, according to people familiar with the matter.

San Francisco-based Decart's software makes it easier for developers to switch between different AI chips, which is increasingly important as companies race to secure more computing power.

A deal could come together soon, the people said, assuming talks don't fall apart or another suitor doesn't emerge.

Decart raised more than $400 million in venture capital funding from investors including Sequoia Capital, Benchmark and Radical Ventures, according to PitchBook. It was valued at nearly $4 billion in May. The company was founded in 2023 by three Israeli engineers, brothers Dean and Orian Leitersdorf and Moshe Shalev. Decart is developing world models known as Lucy and Oasis. World models can generate and simulate real-world 3-D environments.

Amazon developed its own custom AI chip, Trainium3, in competition with Nvidia, which continues to dominate the market for graphics processing units, the specialized chips powering the AI boom. Amazon Web Services gave Decart early access to the chip in 2025 after meeting with the company and being impressed by its founders, The Wall Street Journal has reported.

Amazon's other sizable acquisitions in recent years include a $6.5 billion deal for the MGM movie and television studio in 2022 and a roughly $11 billion deal for Starlink rival Globalstar announced earlier this year.

Israeli news outlet Calcalist reported earlier this month that Decart was in advanced talks to be sold to a major technology company.

背景资料 · CONTEXT原文有

Decart 是一家总部位于旧金山的人工智能初创公司，2023年由三位以色列工程师（莱特斯多夫兄弟迪恩与奥里安、莫舍·沙列夫）创立，其软件帮助开发者在不同AI芯片之间切换——在各公司竞相获取算力的背景下愈发重要。公司正在开发名为 Lucy 和 Oasis 的“世界模型”（world models），可生成并模拟现实世界的3D环境。知情人士称亚马逊的收购估值约为70亿美元，远高于 Decart 今年5月近40亿美元的估值；此前 Decart 已从红杉资本、Benchmark 等投资者处融资超过4亿美元，亚马逊网络服务也在2025年向其开放了自研AI芯片 Trainium3 的早期使用权限。

B1

电动汽车销量下滑带来的积极影响：充电基础设施正在不断完善

P2 · 重点

By Patrick George

在美国，公共充电桩的安装速度依然保持稳定。

Public chargers continue to be installed at a steady clip in the U.S.

美国电动汽车的销量与去年相比下降了一半。显然，电动汽车充电行业并没有意识到这一情况。

美国充电公司今年的公共电动汽车充电桩安装数量，预计将与2025年持平，达到行业历史最高水平。充电公司高管表示，他们计划今年增加数千个新的充电桩，旨在为那些希望更轻松地进行长途旅行的现有电动汽车车主以及涌向二手电动汽车市场的买家提供服务——同时为电动汽车需求可能反弹的那一天做好基础设施准备。

ChargePoint（充电桩公司）首席执行官里克·威尔默表示，在谈及行业低迷期的情绪时说：“我仍觉得大家都还在惊慌失措（往纸袋里喘气）。但增长是真实的。电动汽车用户希望世界上有更多的充电桩。”

自特朗普总统取消旨在鼓励美国人放弃燃油汽车的税收优惠和燃油经济性法规以来，电动汽车行业的乐观情绪一直不足。汽车制造商退出了他们最初雄心勃勃的电动汽车发展目标，取消了现有车型和计划中的车型，并列出了数十亿美元的资产减值，以重新调整其投资并转向燃油汽车。

尽管如此，根据充电行业数据公司Paren的数据，到10月初，美国新增了近2000个充电站，拥有超过12000个充电桩。这一数字与去年大致持平，去年新增了3885个充电站，拥有19839个充电桩。数据显示，美国目前拥有超过8万个公共快速充电桩。

Paren的联合创始人兼首席技术官比尔·费罗表示，充电服务提供商正在从过去的一些错误中吸取教训，这些错误常常让电动汽车车主感到沮丧。

新的充电站平均拥有更多的充电桩，以缩短等待时间。充电桩的功率正在提高，以便更快地“补充”电动汽车的电量。此外，该行业还在努力改善其选址策略：在餐馆和繁忙区域附近增加更多的充电站，而不是设置在远离洗手间或其他便利设施的偏远停车场。

费罗说：“我开始称之为‘伟大的美国充电站建设’。你不会意识到自己已经身处其中，直到你真正参与其中。”

这项建设试图解决电动汽车自诞生之初就面临的“先有鸡还是先有蛋”难题：如果没有足够的充电基础设施，[此处印刷文本如此]2030年美国汽车销量中电动、氢能或插电混动车型的占比……

然而，实际情况是，对这些汽车的需求并未达到汽车行业预测的水平。在税收优惠取消之前，电动汽车的销量在 2025 年第三季度达到峰值，约占新车市场的 12%，此后销量一直徘徊在 6% 左右。

汽车行业高管们经常表示，他们预计美国对电动汽车的需求最终会再次增长，尤其是在价格更实惠的电动车型上市后。

当然，充电服务提供商要想实现持续盈利，还有很长的路要走，因为他们面临着高昂的资本成本。

ChargePoint（充电桩公司）的威尔默指出，公司的亏损正在减少，并且该公司在所有业务领域都实现了增长，包括公共充电站和面向车队的客户。

自动驾驶汽车，其中绝大多数是电动汽车，预计将推动充电需求。

威尔默和其他充电行业高管表示，目前美国的充电桩数量不足以满足现有电动汽车用户的需求，更不用说未来可能加入的更多用户。

充电行业的蓬勃发展并非在所有地区都同步进行。增长主要集中在加利福尼亚州和纽约州等沿海州，尽管费罗表示，德克萨斯州和佛罗里达州的增长速度也在迅速提升。

尽管如此，该行业近期仍有一些成功的案例。Ionna（充电网络）是一家由现代汽车、丰田和其他汽车制造商支持的充电网络，[原文此处有缺失]。更多充电站将由这家零售巨头建设。

特斯拉仍然是该行业的领头羊。根据Paren（充电行业数据公司）的数据，埃隆·马斯克领导的特斯拉公司今年增加了超过3500个快速充电桩，使美国境内的总数达到约4万个。尽管马斯克坚称特斯拉的未来在于机器人技术和人工智能，但该公司仍在不断扩大其充电网络。该公司在其财务报告中并未单独列出充电业务的收入，而是将其列为服务业务的一部分，该业务在第二季度带来了46亿美元的收入。（特斯拉未对置评请求做出回应。）

一些行业高管正在密切关注汽油价格可能如何影响消费者的行为。Tiya Gordon是It's Electric（初创公司）的联合创始人，该公司最近获得了在纽约市建设路边公共充电站的独家合同。她认为，持续上涨的燃料成本可能会导致电动汽车市场出现“摆动”，甚至最近还在身上纹了霍尔木兹海峡的图案。

[图表：自2021年1月以来，美国公共电动汽车快速充电桩数量按月统计。注：数据截至2026年9月。来源：能源部。]

[照片说明：新的充电站拥有更多的充电桩和更强大的充电器，以缩短等待时间。（Angus Mordant/彭博新闻）]

[照片说明：埃隆·马斯克的特斯拉公司今年增加了超过3500个快速充电桩。]

Electric-vehicle sales in the U.S. are down by half compared with last year. The EV charging industry evidently didn't get the memo.

Charging companies in the U.S. are on track to install about as many public EV plugs this year as in 2025, the industry's record high. By adding thousands of new plugs this year, charging executives say they aim to serve longtime EV owners looking for easier road trips as well as buyers flocking to used EVs—while prepping infrastructure for a day when EV demand might bounce back.

"I still think people are breathing into paper bags," Rick Wilmer, chief executive of the charging company ChargePoint, said of the industry's mood amid the downturn. "But the growth is real. The EV-driving community wants more chargers in the world."

Optimism has been in short supply for the EV industry since President Trump axed tax credits and fuel-economy regulations aimed at moving Americans away from gasoline-powered cars. Automakers backed away from their most ambitious pro-EV goals, canceling models and planned ones, while listing multibillion-dollar write-downs as they retool their investments and pivot back to gas.

Even so, the U.S. by early October added nearly 2,000 charging stations with more than 12,000 plugs, according to charging-industry data firm Paren. That is roughly on pace with last year, which ended with 3,885 new charging stations added with 19,839 plugs. The U.S. now has more than 80,000 public fast-charging plugs, data shows.

More crucially, charging providers are learning from the mistakes of the past that often frustrated EV owners, said Bill Ferro, co-founder and chief technical officer of Paren.

New stations have more plugs on average to cut down on wait time. Chargers are becoming more powerful to "refuel" EVs more quickly. And the industry is working on its real-estate game: adding more stations near restaurants and busy areas, not in distant parking lots far from restrooms or other amenities.

"I've started calling it the Great American Build-Out," Ferro said. "You don't realize you're in it until you're in the middle of it."

That build-out attempts to solve a chicken-and-egg problem that beguiled EVs since their earliest days: Without adequate charging infrastructure, [text as printed] U.S. car sales to be electric, hydrogen or plug-in hybrid models by 2030.

Instead, demand for the cars never reached levels projected by the auto industry. EV sales peaked around 12% of the new-car market in the third quarter of 2025 before the tax credits went away, and have hovered around 6% this year.

Auto-industry executives frequently say they expect EV demand to eventually rise again in the U.S., especially as less expensive electric models come to market.

To be sure, charging providers also have a long way to go to reach consistent profitability as they contend with high capital costs.

ChargePoint's Wilmer noted that losses are narrowing and that the company has seen growth across all its business lines, including public stations and fleet customers.

Autonomous vehicles, which are overwhelmingly electric, are expected to drive charging demand.

Wilmer and other charging-industry executives say there aren't enough plugs to properly serve America's current fleet of EV drivers—let alone the ones who might join the fray later on.

The charging space's glow-up isn't happening everywhere. Growth is primarily concentrated in coastal states like California and New York, although Ferro said Texas and Florida are scaling up quickly.

Still, the industry has more than a few recent success stories. Ionna, the charging network backed by Hyundai, Toyota and other automakers, [text fragmented in print] a restroom. More are coming from the retail giant.

The leader by a country mile remains Tesla. According to Paren, the Elon Musk-helmed company added more than 3,500 fast-charging plugs this year, good for about 40,000 total across the U.S. While Musk insists Tesla's future lies in robotics and AI, it continues to scale its charging network. The company doesn't break out charging revenue in its financial reports but lists it as part of a services business that brought in $4.6 billion in the second quarter. (Tesla didn't respond to a request for comment.)

Some industry executives are keeping a close eye on how gas prices might affect consumer behavior. Tiya Gordon, co-founder of It's Electric, a startup that recently secured an exclusive contract to build curbside, public charging stations across New York City, thinks sustained higher fuel costs could mean a "pendulum swing" is coming for EVs—so much so that she recently got a tattoo of the Strait of Hormuz.

[Chart: U.S. public EV fast-charging ports by month since January 2021. Note: Data through September 2026. Source: Energy Department.]

[Photo caption: New stations have more plugs and powerful chargers to cut down on wait time. (Angus Mordant/Bloomberg News)]

[Photo caption: Elon Musk's Tesla added more than 3,500 fast-charging plugs this year.]

B1

达美航空首席执行官表示，公司已做好应对市场波动的准备

P2 · 重点

By Alison Sider

燃油成本正在蚕食达美航空的利润。该公司周五大幅下调了对全年的预期，反映了燃油成本增加了 60 亿美元。

即便燃油价格上涨，达美航空首席执行官埃德·巴斯蒂安（达美航空CEO）表示，他的航空公司——美国盈利能力最强的航空公司——最能应对这种剧烈波动。航空公司减少了航线，并设法提高了机票价格和行李托运费。

到目前为止，旅行需求依然强劲。但一些航空公司今年可能会亏损——与达美航空不同。

《华尔街日报》与巴斯蒂安进行了对话，讨论了行业如何应对当前的挑战，人们对新型高端旅行服务的需求是否会持续增长，以及为什么达美航空坚持不采用星链（SpaceX卫星互联网）Wi-Fi。

《华尔街日报》：上次我们谈话时，似乎大家对燃油价格下降的前景更加乐观。您现在如何看待这个问题？

巴斯蒂安：显然，情况并非如此。很难预测未来会发生什么。因此，我们现在专注于我们可以控制的事情。如果考虑到燃油价格上涨了 60%，但公司的盈利水平与一年前大致相同，这确实令人惊讶，而且与过去燃油价格大幅上涨时的情况截然不同。

《华尔街日报》：您是否注意到人们对更高票价的抵触情绪？是否有人开始放弃旅行？

巴斯蒂安：我不会说人们放弃了旅行，但经济状况较差的人群对价格上涨更为敏感，这就是为什么我们减少了在该市场的航班数量。假日预订情况良好。我们预计第四季度的收入将增长 20%。

《华尔街日报》：高端服务的需求是否存在上限？

埃德·巴斯蒂安：我认为现在还谈不上需求饱和。但仅仅因为有人表示要提供高端服务，并不意味着他们就成了一家高端航空公司。我们已经在这方面努力了 15 年。高端服务指的是整体体验，而不仅仅是更换新的座椅或增加星链服务。我认为，就整体高端服务供给与需求之间的关系而言，我们可能还处于发展的早期或中期阶段。

《华尔街日报》：您提到了星链。当埃隆·马斯克批评你们时，您会感到在意吗？

埃德·巴斯蒂安：我不会去看那些评论。开玩笑归开玩笑，我们清楚自己在做什么，而且我们一直是机上 Wi-Fi 的先驱。星链是一个很棒的产品，我们对它非常了解。我们参与了星链早期在航空领域的应用，但他们的商业模式与我们不同。我们对目前的情况感到满意。

[照片：埃德·巴斯蒂安。（Anindito Mukherjee/彭博社）]

Fuel is taking a chunk out of Delta Air Lines' profits. The carrier on Friday sharply lowered its outlook for the year, reflecting a $6 billion increase in fuel costs.

But even with fuel prices on the rise, Delta Chief Executive Ed Bastian said his airline—the most profitable in the U.S.—is best-positioned to ride out the wild swings. Airlines cut routes and managed to charge higher fares and fees for checked bags.

So far, travel demand has been resilient. But some carriers are on track to lose money this year—unlike Delta.

The Wall Street Journal spoke with Bastian about how the industry is navigating the turbulence, the never-ending appetite for new premium-travel amenities, and why Delta is sticking to its decision to skip Starlink Wi-Fi.

WSJ: Last time we spoke, there seemed to be a lot more optimism that fuel prices were coming down. How do you see things now?

BASTIAN: Well, obviously that didn't come to pass. It's real hard to predict what's going to happen. So what we're doing is focusing on things we can control. If you consider fuel prices were up 60%, and the company earned a level of profitability comparable to where we were a year ago, it's pretty stunning, and certainly very different than anything that you would have seen within the industry going back over a number of cycles of prior fuel spikes.

WSJ: Are you seeing any resistance to higher fares? Are there people who are kind of throwing in the towel?

BASTIAN: I wouldn't say throwing in the towel, but those at the lower end of the economic curve are going to be the most sensitive, and that's why we reduced our supply offering in that market. Holiday bookings look strong. We're expecting our fourth-quarter revenues to be up 20%.

WSJ: Is there an upper limit to premium demand?

BASTIAN: I don't think it's getting crowded. But just because someone says they're going premium, that does not make them a premium airline. We've been at it for 15 years. Premium is the overall experience, not just bringing in new seats or adding Starlink. I think we're probably still in the early-to-mid innings in terms of the overall premium supply versus demand.

WSJ: You mentioned Starlink. Does it get to you when Elon Musk is criticizing you?

BASTIAN: I don't read it. All kidding aside, we know what we're doing in the space, and we've been the pioneer in Wi-Fi. Starlink's a great product, and we know it well. We were involved in the early years of bringing it to aviation, and they had a different business model than we had. We're happy with where we sit.

[Photo: Ed Bastian. (Anindito Mukherjee/Bloomberg News)]

B1

利洁时（英国消费品巨头）在中国社交媒体平台上蓬勃发展

P2 · 重点

By Natasha Khan

几年前，总部位于中国的利洁时（英国消费品巨头）高管们向他们的老板坦诚地表达了自己的看法。这些高管表示，越来越多的消费者不再去超市购物，而是在浏览手机时购买日常家居用品。

他们能否获得一些资金，以便抢先抓住这一趋势？

首席执行官傅乐宏认为，他们所说的可能是中国版的亚马逊和沃尔玛会员计划。但傅乐宏回忆说：“后来我被告知，电子商务已经是一种过时的技术。”

结果是，利洁时调整了其在中国市场的销售策略，从而实现了连续12个季度两位数的销售增长，并帮助这家英国公司应对了成熟市场销售额的放缓。

首席执行官的同事们认为，利洁时需要抓住的是中国市场购物方式的快速转变。在所谓的社交电商模式中，人们在浏览社交媒体应用程序时，看到广告后会顺便购买商品。

利洁时成功地利用了中国消费者购物习惯的转变，这表明，曾经依赖中国市场实现增长的西方公司，即使面临本土竞争对手的挑战，仍然可以在中国市场取得成功。这同时也是社交媒体和购物融合的一个例子。

在利洁时中国团队与傅乐宏进行了一次深入交流后，利洁时全力以赴，进驻了许多中国消费者经常使用的社交媒体应用程序。

该公司在上海郊区的一栋建筑内建立了18个工作室。通过从这些工作室直播的视频，员工在中国流行的社交媒体平台上销售薇婷（脱毛膏品牌）、滴露（Lysol的国际版）以及其他利洁时产品。

在一些工作室中，公司的员工会与客座演员和网红一起，制作关于家庭生活的短喜剧和迷你剧，其中会融入利洁时的品牌。

傅乐宏（利洁时CEO）表示：“这是未来的发展方向，如果不能抓住这一趋势，就会被远远甩在后面。”

中国有14亿人口，其中约有8亿人每天使用抖音等社交媒体平台，抖音是中国版的TikTok，还有快手等应用。利洁时估计，用户平均每天花费约90分钟浏览短视频。这对消费品公司来说至关重要：其中一半的用户已经在这些平台上购买了商品。

社交电商在美国开始流行，购物者现在可以通过点击TikTok和Instagram视频中的链接来购买产品，并通过Whatnot等应用观看直播购物。

利洁时中国市场营销总监黄薇薇表示：“我们都有这样的经历，一天工作结束后，放松地坐在沙发上，观看视频，然后毫不犹豫地点击‘购买’。”

大约在2022年，利洁时中国团队向傅乐宏寻求支持，希望利洁时能够投资社交电商。

最初，团队在招聘具有相关经验的网红或代理机构方面遇到困难，因此员工们决定自己动手。他们将一个会议室改造成一个临时工作室，客户服务人员在视频中回答有关消毒剂化学成分的问题。

销售额大幅增长，这在一定程度上弥补了公司在美国和其他发达市场面临的销售停滞问题。今年上半年，利洁时在新兴市场的销售额增长了8.5%，达到22.3亿英镑。

从该公司位于上海的工作室，团队每天在应用程序上进行12小时的直播，同时，购物者通过聊天功能向他们发送大量评论和问题，聊天内容占据了屏幕的一部分。

利洁时的主持人会迅速做出回应，这就像一种互动式的产品推销。一个团队负责监控直播，为主持人提供指导，并实时调整他们所介绍的产品。

在其他工作室，他们会拍摄短剧和短片。这些视频通常时长不到一分钟，可能会描绘客厅中的场景，或者突出家庭纠纷。其中一个视频中，一位家长与自己的青少年孩子就鞋子的异味问题争论，这时滴露（Dettol）就派上用场了。

去年，该公司的短视频团队为利洁时吸引了4000万新客户，这些客户被他们制作的视频所吸引，该公司表示这些视频的观看次数达到了150亿次。

利洁时预测，社交电商将在其销售产品的100多个国家中以某种形式流行起来，并已开始在包括巴西、墨西哥和马来西亚在内的市场复制其在中国市场的成功模式。

[图片说明：从该公司位于上海的工作室，团队每天在应用程序上进行12小时的直播。]

Several years ago, China-based executives of Lysol maker Reckitt leveled with their boss. More consumers were skipping supermarket runs and buying household staples while they scrolled on phones, the executives said.

Could they have some funds to get ahead of the trend?

Chief Executive Kris Licht figured they were talking about China's equivalents of Amazon.com and Walmart+. "But no," Licht recalled, "I was told that e-commerce was a yesterday technology."

The result: a pivot in Reckitt's China sales tactics, one that propelled 12 consecutive quarters of double-digit sales growth in the country and helped the British firm weather a sales slowdown in established markets.

What the CEO's colleagues said Reckitt needed to jump on was the rapid rise of a different venue for shopping in China. In what's known as social commerce, people scrolling through social-media apps for entertainment end up buying after seeing an ad.

Reckitt's success capitalizing on Chinese consumers' shift points to how Western firms that once counted on China for growth can thrive there though local competitors have made it a tougher place to do business. It's also an example of how social media and shopping are converging.

After its China team's tête-à-tête with Licht, Reckitt pulled out the stops to go to the social-media apps where many Chinese shop.

The company built 18 studios inside a building on the outskirts of Shanghai. On videos broadcast from there, employees sell Veet hair-removal cream, Lysol's international equivalent Dettol and other Reckitt goods on China's popular social-media platforms.

In some of the studios, company staffers join guest actors and influencers making quick comedies and mini-dramas about domestic life featuring a Reckitt brand.

"This is the way forward," Licht said. "Those that don't figure this out will be left behind."

Some 800 million of China's 1.4 billion people now each day use social media like Douyin, the Chinese version of TikTok, and apps like Kuaishou. The users spend an average of about 90 minutes a day scrolling through short videos, Reckitt estimated. Key for consumer-good companies: Half of them have bought items on the platforms.

Social commerce is starting to catch on in the U.S., where shoppers are now buying products by clicking links in TikTok and Instagram videos, and watching live-shopping streams on apps like Whatnot.

"We all have the experience of finishing the day, relaxing on the sofa, watching videos and clicking 'buy' without thinking too much about it," said Vivien Huang, Reckitt's director of marketing in China.

It was around 2022 that Reckitt's team in China sought Licht's support getting Reckitt to invest in social commerce.

The team initially struggled to hire influencers or agencies with the right experience, so staff did it themselves. Out of a conference room converted into a makeshift studio, customer-service staff answered questions on video about the chemical composition of disinfectants.

Sales shot up, boosting the company while it grappled with stagnating sales in the U.S. and other developed markets. Reckitt's emerging-market sales grew 8.5% to 2.23 billion pounds in the first half of this year.

From the company's Shanghai studios, crews broadcast live on the apps for 12 hours a day, while shoppers bombard them with comments and questions in a chat that dominates part of the screen.

The Reckitt hosts respond rapidly, a sort of interactive infomercial. A team monitoring the livestreams guides the hosts and adjusts the featured products they talk about in real-time.

In other studios, short dramas and skits are filmed. The videos, which are often under a minute, might depict a scene from a living room or highlight a domestic dispute. In one, a parent argues with their teenager about smelly shoes—and Dettol comes to the rescue.

The short-form video team drove 40 million new customers to Reckitt last year, drawn by videos they produced that the company said were viewed 15 billion times.

Reckitt predicts social commerce will catch on in some form in the more than 100 other countries where it sells its products, and has begun replicating its China playbook in markets including Brazil, Mexico and Malaysia.

[Photo caption: From the company's Shanghai studios, crews broadcast live on apps for 12 hours a day.]

B1

罗宾汉用户分享他们的财富故事

P2 · 重点

By Hannah Erin Lang and Rachel Mendelson

休斯顿——在罗宾汉（美股券商）首席执行官弗拉德·特内夫（罗宾汉CEO）上台前三十分钟，人群已经沸腾起来。

大约1500名个人投资者挤满了休斯顿会议中心内的座位，他们拍照留念，并摆弄着自己鲜绿色的名牌。他们来自不同的社会阶层——有水管工和小企业主，也有公司中层管理人员和全职家庭主妇。但他们都有一个共同点：他们的投资组合在近几年增长到如此之大，以至于他们辞去了工作，开始全职参与股市交易。

在华尔街，投资者正为油价上涨和全球债券抛售而感到焦虑，这导致美国国债收益率达到二十年来的最高水平。但在罗宾汉的 Hood Summit（罗宾汉峰会）上，派对仍在继续。

罗宾汉为活跃交易者举办的会议是该公司年度活动中规模最大的一次。在为期两天的九月份，数百名普通投资者聚集在迈阿密、拉斯维加斯和休斯顿等城市的会议中心，参加培训课程，交流交易技巧，并在开放式酒吧里畅饮。

本次会议还让人们近距离地观察到金融市场中正在发生的代际转变：自助式投资者在华尔街所扮演的角色比以往任何时候都更加重要。

普通交易者现在可以接触到新的、风险更高的资产，包括事件合约、私人公司、杠杆永续期货，甚至代币化股票。他们推动了市场反弹，并在股价下跌时蜂拥而至，进行买入。

许多人对特斯拉、Alphabet 或 Palantir 等热门科技公司的股票进行了巨额投资——随着人工智能的快速发展，股价不断创下新高，他们的财富也远远超出了他们的想象。

在最近几周，专业的资金管理者一直在应对严峻的债券抛售、不断上涨的燃料成本以及越来越依赖于少数几家科技公司股票的股市上涨。

在休斯顿，没有人为此感到特别担忧。

莎拉·布雷西亚·奥雷尔（Sara Brescia O'Rear）是一位前药剂师，她在40多岁时就退休了，这要归功于她成功的投资。她说：“趋势总是向上，而且会持续向好。我愿意承担风险，因为我知道这样做会带来回报。”

《华尔街日报》花费了两天时间，在 Hood Summit 上与个人投资者进行了交流，询问了他们最冒险的投资举动、他们对当前市场的担忧，以及通过孤注一掷来“改变生活”的意义。

马凯斯·约翰逊（Markese Johnson）说，直到成年后，他才开始考虑购买股票，当时一位儿时的朋友开始向他传授投资技巧，而约翰逊当时正在美国陆军服役。

他的幸运转折点出现在他以大约4美元的价格购买了数千股云计算公司IREN的股票。现在，该股票的交易价格约为35美元。

他说：“这帮助我过上了我从未梦想过的生活。”现在，约翰逊说，他可以负担得起曾经觉得遥不可及的体验，比如去多米尼加共和国度假，或者在华盛顿奇才队的一场比赛中坐在场边。

[交易者简介：马凯斯·约翰逊，36岁，家乡：达拉斯，职业：物流客户协调员，开始交易：2019年；亚历山大·比坦，33岁，家乡：新泽西州恩格尔伍德，职业：企业家，选定的股票：Alphabet、Uber；莎拉·布雷西亚·奥雷尔，41岁，家乡：佐治亚州圣西蒙斯岛，职业：全职交易员，选定的股票：Palantir、罗宾汉；詹姆斯·陈，34岁，家乡：圣地亚哥和底特律，职业：全职交易员，开始交易：2010年；科林·埃德斯曼，36岁，家乡：夏威夷威美亚，职业：全职家庭主夫，选定的股票：GoPro、E.l.f. Beauty，开始交易：2017年；兰斯·扎克热夫斯基二世，37岁（与母亲基蒂），家乡：密歇根州杰克逊，职业：企业主。]

他说：“我希望我的孩子们看到，可以通过正确的方式积累财富。”亚历山大·比坦在2017年通过投资特斯拉股票赚了数百万美元，此后一直热衷于孤注一掷，只投资少数几只股票。他大部分投资仍然集中在几家科技公司。

“如果你看好几只股票，那就是你变得非常富有所需要的一切，”他说。“但你必须判断正确。”六年前，萨拉·布雷西亚·奥雷尔开始购买她能负担得起的尽可能多的 Palantir 股票，最终将她投资组合的约三分之一与该公司的股票挂钩。此后，该股票的涨幅接近 2000%。她称之为“改变人生的财富”。布雷西亚·奥雷尔表示，她现在手头保留了一些现金，但对人工智能可能过度发展所带来的风险并不太担心。“我非常支持科技，”她说。

“从有史以来，每个成功的企业都需要承担债务，”她说。——萨拉·布雷西亚·奥雷尔

詹姆斯·陈出生在中国，大约一年半前失去了工作，并决定全身心投入投资。此后，他通过交易 Alphabet、特斯拉和罗宾汉等股票和期权，获得了大约是他之前工资五倍的收益。“过去，我的目标是在美国企业中取得成功，”他说。他新的目标是什么？“通过交易来实现新的美国梦。”但他不会向其他交易者推荐这种做法。

但在 2024 年，科林·埃德斯曼将他一半的储蓄押在一只股价低于 1 美元的股票上。一年后，这家公司，即矿产供应商美国锑业公司，其股价上涨至两位数。埃德斯曼赚了超过 13,000 美元，并且此后又进行了几次类似的成功交易。“我从来不依赖运气，”他说。“我认为我可以靠自己创造机会。”

2020 年 3 月，兰斯·扎克热夫斯基将他所有的钱投入市场，甚至提前从退休账户中提取资金，以便投入更多资金到科技股和派息股中。在随后的几个月里，他的净资产翻了三倍。他的投资组合稳步增长，让他可以进行一些奢侈消费，例如为他和他的母亲基蒂·扎克热夫斯基预订了一次为期九天的加勒比海游轮，在 Hood Summit 结束后一起旅行。扎克热夫斯基表示，他对市场过度依赖少数几家人工智能公司感到有些担忧。但如果股市突然暴跌，他已经制定了一个计划：“我可能会买入更多。”

[照片说明：上图为 Hood Summit 的与会者聆听罗宾汉首席执行官弗拉德·特内夫在休斯顿发表主题演讲。下图右侧为峰会上的商品。（杰夫·劳滕伯格为《华尔街日报》拍摄）]

HOUSTON—Thirty minutes before Robinhood Markets Chief Executive Vlad Tenev took the stage, the crowd was already buzzing.

Some 1,500 individual investors were packed into the seats of a Houston convention hall, snapping pictures and fiddling with their neon-green nametags. They came from different walks of life—plumbers and small-business owners, mingling with corporate middle managers and stay-at-home parents. But many had something in common: investment portfolios that had grown so large in recent years they had quit their jobs to play the market full time.

Back on Wall Street, investors have been sweating higher oil prices and a global bond selloff that pushed Treasury yields to two-decade highs. But at Robinhood's Hood Summit, the party was still raging.

Robinhood's conference for active traders is the largest of the brokerage's annual events. For two days in September, hundreds of everyday investors descend on a conference center in cities like Miami, Las Vegas and Houston to attend training sessions, swap trading tips and swarm the open bar.

The conference also offers an up-close view of a generational shift in financial markets: The do-it-yourself investor is playing a bigger role on Wall Street than ever before.

Everyday traders have gained access to new, riskier assets, from event contracts and private companies to leveraged perpetual futures and even tokenized stocks. They powered market rebounds, rushing in to buy the dip when share prices swooned.

Many made massive bets on popular tech names such as Tesla, Alphabet or Palantir—and, as the artificial-intelligence rally blew past record after record, got richer than they ever imagined.

In recent weeks, professional money managers have grappled with a punishing bond selloff, rising fuel costs and a stock-market rally that is increasingly dependent on a handful of tech stocks.

In Houston, nobody was all that worried.

"The trend is always going to be up and to the right," said Sara Brescia O'Rear, a former pharmacist who retired in her 40s thanks to her investing success. "I'm willing to take on risk because I know it can pay off."

The Wall Street Journal spent two days talking to individual investors at Hood Summit, asking them about their riskiest investing moves, their concerns about the current market and what it means to make "life-changing money" by betting it all.

Markese Johnson said he never considered buying stocks until well into adulthood, when a childhood friend started showing him the ropes while Johnson was serving in the U.S. Army.

His lucky break came when he bought several thousand shares of cloud computing company IREN when its share price was roughly $4. The stock is now trading around $35.

"It's helping me live a lifestyle I never dreamed I would live," he said. Now, Johnson said he can afford experiences that once felt out of reach, like vacations to the Dominican Republic, or courtside seats to a Washington Wizards game.

[Trader profiles: Markese Johnson, 36, Hometown: Dallas, Occupation: Logistics Account Coordinator, Trading since: 2019; Alexander Bittan, 33, Hometown: Englewood, N.J., Occupation: Entrepreneur, Stock picks: Alphabet, Uber; Sara Brescia O'Rear, 41, Hometown: St. Simons Island, Ga., Occupation: Full-time trader, Stock picks: Palantir, Robinhood; James Chen, 34, Hometown: San Diego and Detroit, Occupation: Full-time trader, Trading since: 2010; Colin Edsman, 36, Hometown: Waimea, Hawaii, Occupation: Stay-at-home dad, Stock picks: GoPro, E.l.f. Beauty, Trading since: 2017; Lance Zakrzewski II, 37 (with mom Kitty), Hometown: Jackson, Mich., Occupation: Business owner.]

["I want [my children] to see you can build wealth the right way," he said. Alexander Bittan made millions betting on Tesla shares back in 2017 and has been a fan of going all-in on just a few stocks ever since. Most of his investments are still tied up in a couple of tech names.]

["If you have a couple high-conviction names, that's all you need to become extremely wealthy," he said. "But you have to be right." Six years ago, Sara Brescia O'Rear started buying as many shares of Palantir as she could afford, eventually tying about one-third of her portfolio to the company's shares. Since then, the stock has surged nearly 2,000%. "Life-changing money," she called it. Brescia O'Rear said she is holding on to a little more cash these days, but the fears of a potential AI overbuild don't keep her up at night. "I'm very pro-technology," she said.]

["Every successful business from the beginning of time—they're going to have to take on debt," she said. — Sara Brescia O'Rear]

[James Chen, who was born in China, lost his job about a year and a half ago and decided to focus on investing full time. Since then, he has made about five times his former salary trading options and stocks like Alphabet, Tesla and Robinhood. "In the past, my goal was to make it in corporate America," he said. His new aim? "Trade myself into the new American dream." It isn't something he would recommend to other traders.]

[But in 2024, Colin Edsman bet half of his savings on a stock trading for less than a dollar a share. A year later, the company, mineral provider United States Antimony, saw its share price climb into the double digits. Edsman made more than $13,000—and has made several similarly successful trades since. "I've never relied on luck," he said. "I figured I could make my own."]

[In March 2020, Lance Zakrzewski put every dollar he had into the market, even taking early withdrawals from his retirement account, to plow more money into tech and dividend-paying stocks. His net worth tripled during the monthslong rebound that followed. The steady gains in his portfolio have allowed for some splurges, like the nine-day Caribbean cruise he booked for him and his mother, Kitty Zakrzewski, once Hood Summit wrapped. Zakrzewski said he is somewhat concerned about the market's reliance on a handful of AI companies. But should stocks suddenly tank, he has a plan: "Probably just buy more."]

BUSINESS NEWS · 4 stories

B3

捷豹推出 Type 01 电动汽车，开启全新发展阶段

P2 · 重点

By Katie Deighton

捷豹推出了一款即将上市的电动汽车，希望这款车能为这家英国汽车制造商开启一个全新的、极致奢华的时代。这款车在设计上有所调整，与近两年前捷豹发布新战略时所展现的那些大胆前卫的元素有所不同。

与捷豹在2024年迈阿密艺术周上发布的粉色和亮蓝色双门概念车相比，四门Type 01车型于上周二以银色外观和“兰花白”内饰亮相。与最初的前卫宣传视频中，面无表情的模特在紫红色背景中走动不同，这款电动汽车的首次广告中，出现了一位英俊的驾驶员、充满氛围的烟雾、高速行驶的汽车，以及以红色和黑色为主的配色方案。

捷豹董事总经理罗登·格洛弗表示：“2024年的那部影片营造了一个概念化的世界，旨在展示这款概念车的全新设计语言和捷豹所要走的方向。”“而现在，这款量产车已经进入现实世界，是真正的人们想要购买的，因此，将其置于一个更贴近现实的背景中显得非常重要。”

预计在美国售价13.05万美元起的 Type 01 车型，仍然是捷豹品牌的一次重大转型。

捷豹以其运动型汽车而闻名，这些汽车在许多人眼中代表着一种优雅、英式潇洒的风格。然而，在过去十年中，捷豹的销量一直不佳。该公司原本计划推出一系列跨界SUV和轿车，以更直接地与宝马和梅赛德斯-奔驰竞争，但这一计划并未成功，管理层也承认该品牌未能与年轻一代的驾驶者建立联系。

因此，该公司制定了一个雄心勃勃的转型计划：它将推翻原有的规则，转而销售更少但价格更高的汽车。在位于英国西米德兰兹的总部，设计师们被要求设计出一种时尚的外形，工程师们则需要将这种外形转化为一种可行的汽车。最终，他们创造出了一款外观未来感十足的纯电动概念车，命名为Type 00，其尺寸比市面上大多数电动汽车都要大。

Type 01车型保留了最初设计中的许多元素。

捷豹路虎外观设计负责人阿列克谢·安德烈耶夫在纽约的一次下午预览活动中，在展示量产车型时表示：“请问问自己，你以前见过像这样的汽车吗？”活动现场用这款新车的各种设计元素进行了装饰，包括黄铜效果的阳极氧化铝样品和“不可能的面板”——一种铝制车身侧面，被塑造成五个不同的平面。

这款量产车型速度也很快，仅需 3.1 秒即可加速到 60 英里/小时。但它是否足够快，能够摆脱围绕它的争议，还有待观察。捷豹发布的预告片和概念车在网上遭到了批评，最终引起了特朗普总统的注意。特朗普在 2025 年的“真相社交”平台上写道：“看了那令人不齿的广告后，谁还想买一辆捷豹？”

演员弗洛伦斯·皮尤（捷豹的最新品牌大使）在周二晚间的一次派对上与汽车设计记者、博主和网络红人进行了交流，以推广 Type 01。邀请名单的制定旨在体现捷豹试图从一款深受年长驾驶者喜爱的汽车，转变为一款受到时尚、国际化的新富阶层青睐的汽车。

过去两年，美国电动汽车市场竞争更加激烈。拜登政府时期提供的 7500 美元的电动汽车税收抵免已经取消，价格上涨，多家汽车制造商取消或推迟了原计划推出的车型。

许多汽车制造商正在努力适应，推出续航里程更长、技术更先进的电动汽车——尤其是在豪华汽车领域。大众汽车旗下的宾利上个月推出了其首款电动汽车，并宣传这款售价 23 万美元的汽车，可在 2.9 秒内从 0 加速到 60 英里/小时。

对于捷豹来说，Type 01 将是美国市场上续航里程较长的电动汽车之一，充满电后可行驶 400 英里。工程师还旨在为汽车爱好者提供他们喜欢的所有燃油汽车的特点，例如模拟发动机声音和加长的引擎盖。

罗登·格洛弗（捷豹董事总经理）表示，说服持怀疑态度的买家和分析师的下一步，将不再侧重于将新款捷豹打造成一家充满活力和创新的公司，而是更多地侧重于解释这款汽车的全部驾驶友好功能。

他说：“如果有些人不喜欢这款汽车，因为它具有两极分化的特点，那也没关系。”“但我们所做的一切，都是为了一个非常明确的目的——我们认为这对捷豹来说是最好的。”

[照片说明：捷豹的 Type 01，于上周二在纽约发布，标志着与之前车型的一次重大转变。阿列克谢·安德烈耶夫（捷豹路虎外观设计负责人），在纽约下午的预览活动中，展示了量产车型。他周围的空间装饰着新款汽车的设计元素，包括黄铜效果的阳极氧化铝样品和“不可能面板”——一种铝制车身侧面，被塑造成五个不同的平面。（Dimitrios Kambouris/Getty Images for Jaguar Land Rover）]

[凯蒂·戴顿为《华尔街日报》领导力研究院的《CMO Today》撰稿。]

Jaguar has introduced the showroom-ready version of the electric vehicle it hopes will usher in a new, ultraluxury era for the British carmaker, leaving aside some of the provocative notes it sounded nearly two years ago when it unveiled its new strategy.

In contrast with the two-door concept car Jaguar unveiled in pink and bright blue at Miami Art Week in 2024, the four-door Type 01 model arrived last Tuesday in silver, with an "orchid white" interior. And where the original avant-garde teaser video depicted expressionless models striding around a fuchsia landscape, the EV's first ad features a handsome driver, atmospheric smoke, cars traveling at high speeds and a color scheme heavy on red and black.

The quirky 2024 film was "a conceptual world for a conceptual car that was really just trying to herald the new design language and the new direction that Jaguar was going in," said Rawdon Glover, Jaguar's managing director. "This is a production car that's in the real world that real people want to buy, and putting it in a context that felt more grounded felt very important."

The Type 01, which is expected to start at $130,500 in the U.S., is nonetheless still a significant departure for the storied Jaguar brand.

Known for its sporty cars that to many represented a suave, English roguishness, Jaguar has been in a sales slump for the better part of the past decade. A plan to compete more directly against the likes of BMW and Mercedes-Benz with a lineup of crossover SUVs and sedans fell flat, and executives conceded the brand has failed to connect with younger drivers.

So the company plotted an ambitious pivot: It would rip up the rulebook and switch to selling fewer cars at a much higher price. At its headquarters in the West Midlands of the U.K., designers were tasked with conjuring up a sleek silhouette that engineers had to transform into a viable machine. The result was a futuristic-looking all-electric concept car dubbed Type 00 that was larger than most EVs on the road.

The Type 01 retains much of the initial attitude.

"Ask yourself the inner question: Have you ever seen a car like this before?" said Alexey Andreev, exterior design lead at Jaguar Land Rover, after the production model was uncovered at an afternoon preview event in New York. The space around him was decorated with the new car's design elements, including samples of brass-effect anodized aluminum and "the impossible panel"—an aluminum body side shaped into five different planes.

The production car is fast, too, able to hit 60 miles an hour in just 3.1 seconds. But whether it's fast enough to outrun its controversies remains to be seen. Jaguar's teaser film and concept car were derided online, eventually catching President Trump's attention. "Who wants to buy a Jaguar after looking at that disgraceful ad?" Trump wrote on Truth Social in 2025.

Actor Florence Pugh, Jaguar's newly minted brand ambassador, schmoozed with design journalists, bloggers and online creators at a party later Tuesday to promote the Type 01. The guest list was crafted to represent Jaguar's attempted leap from being a favored car of older drivers to being considered by the fashionable, cosmopolitan new-money set.

The American electric-vehicle market has grown tougher in the past two years. The Biden-era $7,500 EV tax credit is gone, prices are up, and several automakers have canceled or delayed planned models.

Many automakers are trying to adapt with longer-range, more advanced EVs—especially in the luxury space. Volkswagen-owned Bentley last month unveiled its first EV, touting the $230,000 car's acceleration from 0 to 60 miles an hour in 2.9 seconds.

For Jaguar, the Type 01 will be one of the longer-distance EVs on the U.S. market, capable of driving up to 400 miles on a full charge. Engineers also aimed to give car fanatics all the things they love in a gas-powered vehicle, providing a simulated engine sound and an elongated hood.

The next step in convincing skeptical buyers and analysts will be less about selling the new Jaguar as a youthful, creative company, and more about explaining the car's full driver-friendly capabilities, Glover said.

"If some people don't like the car because it polarizes, that's OK," he said. "But we've done everything we've done with a very, very singular purpose—what we think is best for Jaguar."

[Photo caption: Jaguar's Type 01, unveiled in New York last Tuesday, marks a departure from a previous model. Alexey Andreev, exterior design lead at Jaguar Land Rover, after the production model was uncovered at an afternoon preview event in New York. The space around him was decorated with the new car's design elements, including samples of brass-effect anodized aluminum and "the impossible panel"—an aluminum body side shaped into five different planes. (Dimitrios Kambouris/Getty Images for Jaguar Land Rover)]

[Katie Deighton writes for WSJ Leadership Institute's CMO Today.]

B3

肖恩·费恩声称在全美汽车工人联合会选举中取得压倒性胜利

P2 · 重点

By Christopher Otts

全美汽车工人联合会主席肖恩·费恩及其盟友周五宣布，他们在工会选举中轻松获胜，巩固了费恩在全美最具影响力的劳工组织中的领导地位。

费恩为工会与福特汽车、通用汽车以及斯特兰蒂斯（Jeep-Ram母公司）在2028年就劳工合同进行的下一轮谈判奠定了坚实的基础。全美汽车工人联合会的优先事项将包括寻求恢复传统养老金等福利，并争取在人工智能和自动化取代工厂工作岗位时获得保护。

“全体会员已经表达了他们的意愿，”费恩周五表示。“全美汽车工人联合会的会员团结一致，希望我们继续战斗，并在我们已经取得的成就基础上继续前进。”

虽然尚未公布官方结果，但费恩的竞选团队公布的初步统计数据显示，截至周五下午，他获得了约68%的选票。全美汽车工人联合会的一位发言人表示，官方结果将在稍后公布。

曾以反对费恩的名单参选的全美汽车工人联合会副主席迈克·布斯对《华尔街日报》表示，结果已毋庸置疑。

费恩面临五位竞争对手，其中包括一位曾与他有过矛盾的全美汽车工人联合会副主席。费恩的盟友击败了多位费恩视为其议程绊脚石的全美汽车工人联合会高层官员，赢下了副主席和其他关键职位。

费恩与另一位全美汽车工人联合会副主席里奇·博耶发生了冲突，博耶曾试图挑战费恩，争夺最高职位，但最终失败。他还与负责工会费用的财务主管玛格丽特·莫克发生了矛盾。博耶和莫克未对相关请求做出回应。

费恩曾是印第安纳州科科莫市克莱斯勒工厂的一名电工，后来在工会的底特律总部担任工作人员。他在2023年的决选中以微弱优势赢得了最高职位。他参与了一个临时组建的“改革”团队，旨在带领工会走出腐败丑闻，该丑闻导致两名前全美汽车工人联合会主席被判入狱。这是工会首次允许其会员，而不是一小撮内部人士，直接投票选举其主席和主要领导人。

肖恩·费恩很快成为劳工运动中最受瞩目的领袖之一，他采取了更为强硬的姿态，与底特律的汽车制造商们对抗，并在演讲中使用了粗俗的语言。

在2023年的合同谈判中，肖恩·费恩组织了针对特定汽车工厂的罢工，例如肯塔基州的工厂，该工厂是福特生产其利润最高的卡车的地方。当汽车制造商们让步时，工会获得了大幅的工资增长和其他方面的收益。

肖恩·费恩领导的全美汽车工人联合会实现了长期以来的目标，即组织一家外国汽车制造商的工厂，并在田纳西州查塔努加的大众汽车工厂赢得了工会代表权。但全美汽车工人联合会未能将其成果扩展到其他外国汽车工厂。

在工会位于底特律的总部，肖恩·费恩陷入了内部争议。一位负责监督工会的联邦监察员指控肖恩·费恩对玛格丽特·莫克和里奇·博耶进行了报复。肖恩·费恩不得不恢复他和他领导的委员会之前剥夺的竞争对手的权力，并与他的首席幕僚和主要战略家分道扬镳。

[照片：肖恩·费恩。（何塞·胡瓦雷斯/美联社）]

United Auto Workers President Shawn Fain and his allies on Friday claimed an easy victory in the union's election, cementing Fain's leadership of one of the most powerful forces in American organized labor.

Fain secured a position of strength for the union's next showdown with Ford Motor, General Motors and Jeep-Ram parent Stellantis in 2028 over labor contracts. The UAW priorities will include seeking to restore benefits such as traditional pensions and winning protections against artificial intelligence and automation replacing factory jobs.

"The membership has spoken," Fain said Friday. "UAW members are united and want us to keep fighting and building on what we've done."

While official results weren't available, tallies posted by Fain's campaign showed his garnering about 68% of the vote as of Friday afternoon. A UAW spokesman said official results would come later.

UAW Vice President Mike Booth, who ran on a ticket opposing Fain, told The Wall Street Journal that there is no doubt about the outcome.

Fain faced five challengers, including a UAW vice president with whom he had feuded. Fain's allies defeated top UAW officials whom Fain viewed as impediments to his agenda for vice-presidential slots and other key roles.

Fain clashed with another UAW vice president, Rich Boyer, who unsuccessfully challenged Fain for the top job. He feuded with the person who signs the checks for union expenses, Secretary-Treasurer Margaret Mock. Boyer and Mock didn't return a request for comment.

Fain, a former Chrysler plant electrician from Kokomo, Ind., had been a staffer at the union's Detroit headquarters when he narrowly won the top job in a runoff election in 2023. He was part of a hastily arranged "reform" slate meant to steer the union past a corruption scandal that sent two former UAW presidents to federal prison. It was the first time the union allowed its members, rather than a cadre of insiders, to directly vote on its president and top leaders.

Fain soon became one of the labor movement's most recognizable figures, adopting a more confrontational posture with the Detroit automakers, hurling profanities during speeches.

During contract talks in 2023, Fain called targeted strikes of certain auto plants, such as the Kentucky facility where Ford produces its most profitable trucks. The union secured large wage increases and other gains when the carmakers relented.

Fain's UAW then realized a long-sought goal of organizing a foreign automaker's plant, winning union representation at Volkswagen's factory in Chattanooga, Tenn. But the UAW has been unable to extend its wins to other foreign auto plants.

At the union's home base in Detroit, Fain has been bogged down in internal controversies. A federal monitor charged with overseeing the union accused Fain of retaliating against Mock and Boyer. Fain has had to restore powers he and the board had stripped from his rivals and part ways with his chief of staff and top strategist.

[Photo: Shawn Fain. (Jose Juarez/AP)]

B3

关税退款助推品牌营销投入

P2 · 重点

By Patrick Coffee

从梅西百货到李维斯，多家公司已宣布，它们从联邦政府收到的数百万美元关税退款中的一部分，将用于增加营销支出，以应对即将到来的假日购物季。

这些表态可能更多反映的是各公司的投资者关系策略，而非营销计划。

今年秋季，眼镜制造商Warby Parker（眼镜品牌）将推出Intelligent Eyewear（AI眼镜），这是一款与谷歌和三星合作开发的、搭载人工智能技术的眼镜系列。

为了帮助推广这款重要产品，该公司将利用上个季度收到的1180万美元，这是美国最高法院推翻特朗普总统对进口商品征收的关税后获得的。

Warby Parker首席财务官阿德里安·米切尔（Warby Parker CFO）在公司8月份的财报电话会议上表示，这笔资金的注入使该公司能够通过抵消其他费用，更灵活地投资于其品牌。

米切尔表示：“在营销方面，我们计划大幅增加对品牌和媒体的整体投资，相关投入将由我们与合作伙伴共同承担，以扩大Intelligent Eyewear的推广活动。”“关于对业务的投资以及整合Intelligent Eyewear——这些都将完全由关税带来的收益来资助。”

数十亿美元的资金已经开始流向符合退款资格的一些进口商，此前美国最高法院在二月份做出了裁决。

据美国海关与边境保护局的一位发言人称，截至10月2日，该机构已收到约1360亿美元的退款申请，并已将1260亿美元转交给财政部进行支付。根据9月份的一份法庭文件，超过28.6万家进口商申请了退款。

随后的许多讨论都集中在，获得关税退款的公司是否会降低价格，但一些公司已告知投资者，部分资金将用于促销和品牌投资。

e.l.f.美妆将利用迄今为止收到的约5000万美元中的一部分，以提高其品牌在英国、加拿大和德国等主要市场的知名度。该公司表示，在截至明年3月的财政年度中，其整体营销支出将达到23%至25%净销售额的较高水平，这部分得益于退款。

上周三，李维斯公司表示，其大部分关税退款将用于节日季的营销活动。“我们重新分配了8000万美元关税退款中的6000万美元，”李维斯公司的首席财务与增长官哈米特·辛格在财报电话会议上表示。“我认为，其中三分之一用于营销，三分之一用于分销和物流，还有三分之一用于促销活动。”

[帕特里克·科菲为《华尔街日报》领导力研究院的《CMO Today》撰稿。]

Advertisers from Macy's to Levi Strauss have announced that a portion of the multimillion-dollar tariff refunds they received from the federal government will go to boost marketing spending as the holiday shopping season approaches.

These messages may say more about the companies' investor-relations strategies than their marketing plans.

Later this fall, eyeglass maker Warby Parker will launch Intelligent Eyewear, a line of AI-powered glasses developed in partnership with Google and Samsung.

In order to help market this critical product rollout, the company will draw on the $11.8 million it received last quarter after the Supreme Court struck down President Trump's tariffs on imported goods.

The cash injection has given Warby Parker flexibility to invest more in its brand by offsetting other expenses, Chief Financial Officer Adrian Mitchell said on the company's August earnings call.

"Within marketing, we plan to meaningfully increase our total brand and media investments, which will be shared with our partners as we scale Intelligent Eyewear campaigns," Mitchell said. "With regards to the investments in the business and integrating Intelligent Eyewear—that's fully funded by the tariff benefits."

Billions of dollars have begun making their way to some of the importers that qualified for refunds after the February high court decision.

As of Oct. 2, U.S. Customs and Border Protection has received approximately $136 billion in refund requests and sent $126 billion to the Treasury Department for payment, said an agency spokesperson. More than 286,000 importers requested refunds, according to a September court filing.

Much subsequent conversation has focused on whether companies that receive tariff refunds would cut prices, but some recipients have told investors that portions will go toward promotions and brand investments.

E.l.f. Beauty will use some of the approximately $50 million it has received so far to help raise awareness of its brands in key markets such as the U.K., Canada and Germany. The company's overall marketing spending for the fiscal year through next March will skew toward the high end of a given range of 23% to 25% of net sales, powered in part by the refunds.

Levi Strauss last Wednesday said a large portion of its tariff refunds will go toward holiday-season marketing campaigns. "We redeployed $60 million out of the $80-odd million from tariffs that we received," Harmit Singh, the denim-maker's chief financial and growth officer, said on its earnings call. "I would say a third is marketing, a third is distribution and logistics, and a third is promotional activities."

[Patrick Coffee writes for WSJ Leadership Institute's CMO Today.]

B3

以得来速著称的咖啡连锁 Dutch Bros 欲吸引步行顾客

P2 · 重点

By Kristin Broughton

Dutch Bros（美国咖啡连锁）是一家发展迅速的、以提供色彩鲜艳的饮品和热情友好的咖啡师而闻名的得来速咖啡连锁，其吸引步行客流的策略是：开设仅接待步行顾客的窗口店。

该公司去年11月在洛杉矶南加州大学附近开设了首家仅接待步行顾客的门店，并将其描述为对这一概念的测试。这家门店设有三个窗口，分别用于点餐、取餐和移动订单，直到今年5月，它的销售额一直高于所有其他Dutch Bros咖啡店，之后被芝加哥地区第一家门店的开业所超越，这是Dutch Bros首席财务官乔希·根瑟（Dutch Bros CFO）所说。根瑟表示，Dutch Bros现在正在考虑在其他人口稠密的地区开设类似的步行顾客门店。

根瑟说：“我们将寻找其他可以进行测试的地点，以了解这是否是一种吸引顾客的新方式，从而拓展不同的商业地产。”

此举表明，随着Dutch Bros在全国范围内扩张，它正在适应新的市场。

总部位于亚利桑那州坦佩的Dutch Bros成立于1992年，位于俄勒冈州南部。近年来，该公司发展迅速，从其在太平洋西北部的根基开始，向东扩张。该公司五年前上市，截至6月30日，拥有1225家门店，比去年增加了17%。其目标是在2029年将门店数量增加到2029家。

在不断增长的、提供含咖啡因、可定制饮品的市场中，Dutch Bros以其对客户服务的重视而脱颖而出，这是行业分析师的观点。当顾客开始排队时，公司的迎宾员会在顾客的车窗前接受点单。公司鼓励咖啡师（公司称之为“broistas”）通过闲聊和播放音乐来营造一种愉快的体验。

Dutch Bros还以其色彩鲜艳的能量饮料而闻名，提供多种口味和配料，并且主要提供冷饮。该公司的外卖店设有外带窗口，用于移动订单的取餐，店内没有供顾客休息的区域。

分析师表示，尽管通货膨胀给家庭预算带来了压力，但咖啡连锁店在很大程度上不受餐饮业其他领域的压力影响。

萨朗·沃拉（Telsey Advisory分析师）说：“这仍然是一种负担得起的奢侈品。”

Dutch Bros拥有大部分门店，并利用其运营收入来资助其发展。最新季度（截至6月30日）的收入为5.509亿美元，比去年同期增长了32%。利润增长了46%，达到3740万美元。

收购是该公司扩张战略的一部分。Dutch Bros支付了约 2000 万美元，收购了 Clutch Coffee（卡罗来纳地区咖啡连锁）的 20 家门店。乔希·根瑟（Dutch Bros CFO）表示：“这是我们获取房产的另一种方式，在某些情况下，它还能加速我们进入某个市场或在现有市场开设门店。”

分析师表示，来自竞争对手的压力正在增加，其中包括一家名为 7 Brew（咖啡连锁）的私营连锁咖啡店，以及星巴克等大型企业，星巴克目前正在进行转型，并对其饮品菜单进行更新。阿里·费尔汉德勒（晨星分析师）表示，这可能会对 Dutch Bros雄心勃勃的增长目标产生不利影响，他预计该公司到 2029 年的门店数量将比目标少约 300 家。

从长远来看，Dutch Bros的目标是在全国范围内开设多达 7000 家门店。分析师表示，现在就判断增加更多仅提供外带服务的窗口是否能扩大该公司的潜在市场还为时过早。

[图片说明：这家咖啡连锁店去年 11 月在洛杉矶开设了首家仅接待步行顾客的门店。]

[克里斯汀·布罗顿为《华尔街日报》领导力研究所的首席财务官杂志撰稿。]

Dutch Bros, the fast-growing, drive-through coffee chain known for its colorful drinks and outgoing baristas, has a strategy to attract pedestrian traffic: walk-up-only windows.

The company opened its first walk-up-only location last November near the University of Southern California in Los Angeles, in what it describes as a test of the concept. The location—which features three windows for ordering, pickup and mobile orders—had the highest sales volume of any Dutch Bros coffee shop until May, when it was surpassed by the opening of the first Chicago-area location, according to Chief Financial Officer Josh Guenser. Dutch Bros is now considering opening similar walk-up shops in other densely populated areas, Guenser said.

"We will be looking for other walk-up tests, to see if that's a new way to go after different real estate," Guenser said.

The move shows how Dutch Bros is adapting to new markets as it expands nationwide.

Tempe, Ariz.-based Dutch Bros, founded in southern Oregon in 1992, has been on a tear in recent years, expanding from its Pacific Northwest roots eastward. The company, which went public five years ago, operated 1,225 shops as of June 30, an increase of 17% from last year. It aims to boost its shop count to 2,029 in 2029.

In the growing market for caffeinated, customizable drinks, Dutch Bros stands apart for its focus on customer service, according to industry analysts. Company greeters take customer orders at their car windows when lines begin to form. Baristas—which the company calls "broistas"—are encouraged to create a fun experience by making small talk and playing music.

Dutch Bros is also known for brightly colored energy drinks, offering a long list of flavors and add-ons, and for serving mostly cold drinks. The company's drive-through locations have walk-up windows for mobile-order pickups and no cafe space for lingering inside.

While inflation has put pressure on household budgets, coffee chains have been mostly immune from the pressures elsewhere in the restaurant industry, analysts said.

"It's still an affordable luxury," said Sarang Vora, an analyst with Telsey Advisory Group.

Dutch Bros, which owns most of its shops, is using cash from its operations to fund its growth. Revenue during the latest quarter ended June 30 was $550.9 million, up 32% from a year earlier. Profit rose 46% to $37.4 million.

Acquisitions are part of the company's expansion strategy. Dutch Bros paid about $20 million to acquire 20 locations from Clutch Coffee, a regional coffee chain in the Carolinas. "This is another way for us to get a hold of real estate, and in some cases accelerate our opening into a market, or opening of shops in an existing market," said Guenser.

Competition from rivals is increasing, including from 7 Brew, a privately held drive-through coffee chain, and larger players such as Starbucks, which is undergoing a turnaround and refreshing its drink menu, analysts said. That could put a damper on Dutch Bros's ambitious growth targets, said Ari Felhandler, an analyst with Morningstar who anticipates the company will fall short of its 2029 shop count goal by about 300 locations.

Over the long term, Dutch Bros has a goal of operating as many as 7,000 drive-through locations nationwide. It is too early to say whether the addition of more walk-up-only windows could increase the company's addressable market, analysts said.

[Photo caption: The coffee chain opened its first walk-up-only location last November in Los Angeles.]

[Kristin Broughton writes for the WSJ Leadership Institute's CFO Journal.]

B7

投资者的头号担忧

P2 · 重点

By Cristina Roca

法国债券市场崩盘引发的连锁反应风险令投资者感到不安。

但如果说明年市场面临的风险，投资者更应该关注的是其他更重要的问题——至少根据德意志银行外汇研究主管乔治·萨拉维洛斯（德银外汇研究主管）的说法，他本周总结了与客户的对话，并表示：

“明年市场面临的最大系统性风险不是法国，而是人工智能生态系统中可能出现的‘问题’：例如安全事件、首次公开募股失败或营收未达预期。”

萨拉维洛斯引用了一份布鲁金斯学会的研究报告，该报告最近估计，到2032年，人工智能实验室需要产生超过3.5万亿美元的年收入，才能获得数据中心投资的回报。

Investors were rattled by contagion fears over a bond-market meltdown in France.

But when it comes to risks looming over the market next year, they have bigger things to worry about—at least according to George Saravelos, head of foreign-exchange research at Deutsche Bank, who summarized his conversations with clients this week like this:

"The biggest systemic risk in the market next year is not France but 'something going wrong' in the AI ecosystem: a safety event, a failed IPO, or disappointing revenues."

Saravelos cited a Brookings paper that recently estimated AI labs need to generate upward of $3.5 trillion a year by 2032 to earn a return on data-center investments.

On this day in 1982, after nearly 17 years of going nowhere, the Dow Jones Industrial Average finally rose above the peak it set in the bull market of the 1960s.

P3 · 延伸

The WSJ Dollar Index, which tracks the greenback against a basket of currencies, finished its fourth consecutive week of gains Friday, the longest streak since May 2025, according to Dow Jones Market Data. The gains reflect investor expectations that the Federal Reserve will continue to raise interest rates. Higher rates tend to boost the dollar by making dollar-denominated assets more attractive.

街谈巷议

HEARD ON THE STREET · 1 stories

B7

住宅建筑商与卖家争抢购房者

P2 · 重点

竞争加剧将迫使莱纳（美国住宅建筑商）、KB Home（美国住宅建筑商）等公司加大激励力度

Competition is heating up, which will force companies like Lennar and KB Home to beef up incentives

现在，随着抵押贷款利率超过 7%，您是否准备好对房屋的价值进行更现实的评估？建筑商可能不会因此感谢您。

在过去的四年里，房屋建筑商一直受益于一个有利因素：普通房屋的出售者不愿降低价格，因此现有房屋市场的竞争压力较小。

但这种情况正在发生变化，这将进一步削弱建筑商本已缩小的利润空间。

莱纳和 KB Home最近表示，他们面临着来自普通房屋出售者的更强竞争，这些出售者更有动力出售房屋。莱纳在截至 8 月的三个月内，其西部地区的房屋平均售价比去年同期下降了 44,000 美元，降幅为 7%。其中部地区的平均价格比一年前下降了 5%。该公司表示，来自现有房屋市场的更强竞争是导致价格下降的一个因素。

KB Home将其第四季度平均售价的预估中点从 50 万美元下调至 48 万美元。南加州地区的房屋出售者正在降低价格以吸引买家，因此 KB Home必须跟上，否则可能会错失销售机会。

Redfin（房产平台）的数据显示，在截至 9 月 20 日的四周内，现有房屋市场中 21% 的出售者降低了他们的要价。最显著的变化体现在让步方面，而这些让步不会反映在标价中。

8 月，45% 的房屋出售者向买家提供了让步，例如提供支付交易成本或向买家提供维修费用。在亚特兰大和田纳西州纳什维尔等供应过剩的“阳光地带”市场中，现在有 70% 的出售者提供让步。这些市场也是房屋建筑商通常聚集的地方。

直接降价的情况可能会变得更加普遍，因为待售房屋的数量几乎恢复到了疫情前的水平。Realtor.com（房产平台）的数据显示，9 月份的库存量比 2019 年同期的水平低 5%。

疫情期间的房地产繁荣打破了新建房屋市场和现有房屋市场之间通常的竞争动态。去年，现有房屋的销售量比 2022 年的水平下降了五分之一。

四年前，抵押贷款利率开始上升后，那些拥有超低利率的房主选择继续持有房屋，导致待售房屋的供应量减少。而且，许多出售者都抱着“这是我的价格，如果你们想买，就出这个价”的心态。他们宁愿将房屋从市场上撤下，也不愿接受较低的报价。

这对建筑商来说非常有利。

[印刷版继续，但在提取内容中被截断：] 缺点是，这种“利率买断”方式成本高昂，并降低了利润空间。莱纳目前必须提供诸如“利率买断”之类的激励措施，其价值相当于房屋价值的 12%，才能促成交易。在截至 8 月的三个月里，该公司的毛利率为 15.8%，回到了 2008-09 年金融危机之后的水准。情况可能会变得更糟。更高的[文本结束]

[图表：新建房屋销售激励措施占收入的百分比，2009-2026 年。注：由于新冠疫情，2019 年第四季度至 2020 年第四季度的数据不可用。来源：John Burns Research & Consulting。]

[照片：David Paul Morris/彭博新闻社]

Ready to be realistic about the value of your home now that mortgage rates are above 7%? Builders won't thank you for it.

Home builders had one thing working in their favor over the past four years: Regular home sellers weren't willing to budge on prices, so competition from the existing home market was low.

This is starting to change, which will hurt builders' already diminished profit margins.

Lennar and KB Home said recently that they are facing tougher competition from regular owners who are more motivated to sell. The average price Lennar sold homes for in its west region over the three months through August was $44,000 lower than the same period last year—a 7% drop. Average prices in its central region dropped 5% compared with a year ago. It said stiffer competition from the existing-home market was one factor behind the declines.

KB Home lowered the midpoint of its average sales-price estimate for the fourth quarter from $500,000 to $480,000. Southern Californian home sellers are cutting their prices to entice buyers, so KB Home has to follow or risk missing out on sales.

In the four weeks through Sept. 20, 21% of sellers in the existing-home market dropped their asking price, data from Redfin shows. The most significant activity is happening with concessions, which don't show up in headline prices.

In August, 45% of home sellers gave concessions to buyers, such as offering to cover the closing costs or handing the buyer money for repairs. In oversupplied Sunbelt markets such as Atlanta and Nashville, Tenn., 7 in 10 sellers are now offering concessions. These are the same markets where home builders tend to cluster.

Outright price cuts are likely to become more common because the number of homes for sale is almost back to prepandemic levels. In September, inventory was 5% below the same month of 2019, according to data from Realtor.com.

The pandemic-era housing boom sent the usual competitive dynamic between the new-home and existing-home markets haywire. Last year, sales of existing homes were one-fifth below 2022 levels.

Once mortgage rates began to rise four years ago, owners with ultralow rates stayed put, starving the for-sale market of supply. And many sellers had a "here is my price, make me move" mentality. They preferred to take their property off the market rather than accept a lower offer.

That suited builders nicely.

[Print edition continues but is cut off in extraction after:] The downside is that the buydowns are expensive and have sapped profit margins. Lennar currently has to offer incentives such as rate buydowns worth 12% of the value of a home to get deals done. In the three months through August, the company's gross margin was 15.8%—back at levels last seen in the aftermath of the 2008-09 financial crisis. Things might get worse. Higher [text ends]

封闭式基金 · B6

Listed are the 300 largest closed-end funds as measured by assets. Closed-end funds sell a limited number of shares and invest the proceeds in securities. Unlike open-end funds, closed-ends generally do not buy their shares back from investors who wish to cash in their holdings. Instead, fund shares trade on a stock exchange. NA signifies that the information is not available or not applicable. NS signifies funds not in existence for the entire period. 12 month yield is computed by dividing income dividends paid (during the previous 12 months for periods ending at month-end or during the previous 52 weeks for periods ending at any time other than month-end) by the latest month-end market price adjusted for capital gains distributions. Depending on the fund category, either 12-month yield or total return is listed.

股价表现：无线公司的艰难一日 · B7

Share-price performance chart (intraday, 10 a.m. to 4 p.m.): AT&T, Verizon, T-Mobile US.Shares in AT&T, Verizon Communications and T-Mobile US fell sharply Friday in the wake of SpaceX's $8 billion spectrum deal. AT&T and Verizon suffered their biggest one-day declines in more than 24 years. For T-Mobile, it was the worst percentage drop in more than 13 years. AT&T closed down 9.8%, Verizon fell 8.7% and T-Mobile declined 13%.Source: FactSet.
